# About this item

*    LIFETIME WARRANTY WITH SUNEXPRESS: Get in the express lane with the industry’s leading warranty program and exchange warranted products directly with Sunex. 
*    CR-MO STEEL: Provides the strength and flexibility to handle high torque impact tools. 
*    RADIUS CORNER DESIGN: Reduces wear and increases torque on fasteners by placing force on fastener walls rather than fastener corners. 
*    CHAMFERED OPENING: Quickly aligns fastener to socket. 
*    MEETS ANSI STANDARDS: All sockets are rigorously tested, ensuring peak performance and durability.

## Product Description

The Sunex 2695 1/2” Drive 12 Point Driveline Limited Clearance Socket Set includes metric sizes from 8mm to 16mm. They are designed to remove and install driveline bolts, torque converters, cylinder heads, and 4x4 transmission casings. Each socket has been forged from high grade Cr-Mo steel, designed with radius corners, and boldly laser marked. All of this ensures our sockets’ professional-level performance conveniently stored in one heavy duty storage case. Meets ANSI Standards.

[How customer reviews and ratings work](javascript:void(0))

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.

### Customers say

Customers find the socket set offers great value for money and appreciate its durability, noting that the extensions and wobble adapter have held up well. The set works effectively, with one customer specifically mentioning its compatibility with Makita impacts, and comes in a nice blow-molded case that keeps everything organized. While the set includes all necessary sizes, some customers find the printed sizes difficult to read, and opinions about the sockets are mixed.

_5 out of 5 stars_

##### Great Affordable Choice For Impact Wrench

A great socket set that encompasses a wide array of metric sizes. Sockets came oiled and feel very high quality. I'm not a professional mechanics, but I use them quite a bit, they seems extremely solid. I love the etching on them, and the rough material is nice for gripping when your hands are greasy. The case is a nice addition and feels fairly solid, sockets do not move around. I was worried that being so square and not beveled that they would not not fit onto my impact wrench since it's a hog ring style anvil. This was not an issues and they wiggle right on with no problem. Great kit, great price. Seems like they will last a lifetime with no problem.

Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. I bought this set as I did not have any deep well 1/2in drive metric impact sockets, and my shallow set was lacking on the size range.

This set has a very nice selection of sizes. I have been using them for a few weeks now and they are holding up great, barely a sign of use on them. The case is nice, but sadly would not fit in my tool box. I found one of the plastic holders to stand them all up in a row and keep the box under the workbench in case I ever need it.

As others have said, the anti rust oil is a little smelly and a pain to clean off. However I will not complain about it as I would rather have oily and new sockets, than dry rusty ones.

My only tiny gripe I could have, is I think going down to 9mm is a little too small for a 1/2 drive. I think a better choice in size range would have started at 13mm and went to 36mm. But that is just in my range of use, other people might actually use those smaller sockets. If I need something that small, I use my 3/8 impact and sockets.

Otherwise a excellent set and I highly recommend them.       [Read more Read less](javascript:void(0))       7 people found this helpful Helpful  Sending feedback...Thank you for your feedback. *   [![Image 107](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif) AZNEIL](https://www.amazon.com/gp/profile/amzn1.account.AHEMFVXZBZV5LGHXV5W6IFQY5JEQ?ref=cm_cr_dp_d_bdcrb_top) _5 out of 5 stars_ ##### [Stand a little bit farther away than normal when you open the case.](https://www.amazon.com/portal/customer-reviews/srp/-/RZKFJPMFXT1MJ/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_ttl?_encoding=UTF8&ie=UTF8)Reviewed in the United States on October 11, 2015 [Size: 43-piece Metric](https://www.amazon.com/portal/customer-reviews/B00MPQ88RY/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_fmt?_encoding=UTF8&formatType=current_format)[Verified Purchase](https://www.amazon.com/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_avp?ie=UTF8&nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA)  Brief content visible, double tap to read full content. Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. As already mentioned by another reviewer, when I opened up the top of this, the oil used on the sockets emitted a really nasty smell. It made me sneeze, it was so bad. Well, this weekend, I took it outside, and cleaned the 'factory' oil off with carburetor cleaner, and then re-applied a coat of motor-oil. (If you do this, make sure you have the proper personal protective equipment [gloves etc.] to yourself safe from the chemicals.) The sockets looked kind of 'dry' and 'frosty' after they dried off from the solvent. But after the coat of motor oil, the 'sheen' came right back. I was happy to see that the lettering on the sockets remained unaffected by the solvent. I also wiped the case out with some paper towels, and noticed that some of the 'red' from the plastic case came off with the oil. Also, there was a red stain in the bottom of my cleaning pan, which indicates that the original 'factory' oil had reacted adversely with the case, which left me questioning if the smell was from the oil or the case, or the combination.

Other than that issue, the case is built tough. It has two black latches on the front of the case, but also it has two additional latches on the side of the case. If you aren't aware of the side latches, a little bit of extra exuberance using the black tabs to remove the top without unlatching the sides will leave you with one or two black tabs in you hands, if you try to use them to pull the case top open. (they snap back on).

The sockets and fittings are built tough. I was especially impressed with the Universal Joint, or Knuckle. (you can purchase the universal joint separate if you like). You will know when you pick the case of sockets that there is some real metal in there. I really liked that there is a 'complete set' here including a really good selection of extensions and a universal joint.

The case came packed in a box about twice as thick as the case, with some really good packing, so it arrived in fine condition.

Considering the 'nasty smelling oil' and red stuff coming off from the case issue, which I would take half a star off for that. But you can't do a 'half star', a "5" is how I feel about the whole package. # Product Summary: Sunex 2569, 1/2" Drive Master Impact Socket Set, 43Piece, Metric, 9mm - 30mm, Standard/Deep, Cr-Mo Alloy Steel, Radius Corner Design, Heavy Duty Storage Case, Universal Joint & Impact Extensions

From Sunex Tools

4.8 out of 5 stars, 1,414 ratings

[Customer reviews](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B00MPQ88RY?ref=dp_product_quick_view#customer-reviews_feature_div)
100+ bought in past month

## Price

One-time purchase: $144.79

## About this Item

*   LIFETIME WARRANTY WITH SUNEXPRESS: Get in the express lane with the industry’s leading warranty program and exchange warranted products directly with Sunex.
*   CR-MO STEEL: Provides the strength and flexibility to handle high torque impact tools.
*   RADIUS CORNER DESIGN: Reduces wear and increases torque on fasteners by placing force on fastener walls rather than fastener corners.
*   CHAMFERED OPENING: Quickly aligns fastener to socket.
*   MEETS ANSI STANDARDS: All sockets are rigorously tested, ensuring peak performance and durability.

## Product Description

 The Sunex 2695 1/2” Drive 12 Point Driveline Limited Clearance Socket Set includes metric sizes from 8mm to 16mm. They are designed to remove and install driveline bolts, torque converters, cylinder heads, and 4x4 transmission casings. Each socket has been forged from high grade Cr-Mo steel, designed with radius corners, and boldly laser marked. All of this ensures our sockets’ professional-level performance conveniently stored in one heavy duty storage case. Meets ANSI Standards.
